We can implement a dedicated LP search:
```
def _lp_bundle_search(a, L, m, n, rng, time_limit):
   a=n cos
   history constraints: list of active lag indices (unique) maybe up to 200-500.
   Init active = top 50 lags.
   M=score.
   while time:
       c=conv(a); M=max
       # update active: include top K of current c and near max
       active = top K or c[M - delta]
       solve LP max t s.t. 2(a*δ)_k + t ≤0 for k in active, sum δ=0, |δ_i|≤bound*a_i, |t|≤bound
       if t>0: δ = x; line search λ fractions; accept best exact score
       else: # no decreasing direction for active; try different active set/rng/restart
```

Potential problem with many variables and constraints: HiGHS with 8000 variables, 100 constraints may still be slow because dense. We can use a smaller dimension: block/cluster sequence into blocks of size B (e.g., 8 or 16) for LP direction, solve δ constant per block, then smooth. But if optimum needs fine features, full for final. We can use block LP for many iterations, full LP occasional.

Let's design final code in detail.

Functions:
- `normalize(a, lower=None)`: copy, clip nonfinite/negative, sum, if sum<eps fill ones, else divide. Optionally enforce sum.
- `fft_convolution(a, L_cache?)`: takes a normalized length n, uses global L,m; compute FFT? For repeated, we can precompute? a changes. Use `np.fft.rfft(a, L)` and square. Ensure real. Clip small negative to 0? For max, tiny negative irrelevant; but for weights, clip. Use `np.maximum(conv,0,out=conv)`. Return conv.
- `score_conv(c,n)`: `2*n*max(c)` if sum=1. Actually if sum=1 exactly. For safety: score = 2*n*max(c)/(sum^2). We'll maintain sum1 but can use sum.
- `soft_weights(c, M, temp, K)`: return weights over m. We can use hybrid:
   - `w = np.zeros(m)`. Let threshold = M - max(temp_abs, 1e-6*M). But if temp corresponds to objective scale? We'll use scaled `v = n*c`, Mv = max(v) ≈1.5. Use v. threshold = Mv - delta, delta maybe annealed (0.05,0.02,0.005). Weights = (v - threshold)_+ normalized, plus small baseline over top K to avoid all zero. For gradient, weights should approximate subgradient of max: uniform over lags where v close to max. Use `w_k = exp((v_k - Mv)/tau)` with tau; equivalent. We can use tau = delta (0.01,0.003). For numerical, `z=(v-Mv)/tau; z clip [-50,0]; w=exp(z); w/=sum`. This gives more weight to max. For early coarse tau=0.1, final tau=0.005. This is better than previous temperature v temp cap.
- `gradient_direction(a, w, L, m, n)`: Frev cached? a changes, so recompute `F = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)` each time? It is same cost as conv. Could combine: we need conv a*a and grad using F. Gradient: `W = np.fft.rfft(w, L); g = np.fft.irfft(W * F, L)[n-1:m]`; clip >=0. Then maybe normalize `g` by max to set step scale.
- Multiplicative update: `a2 = a * exp(-eta * g)`. But g values are ~ ∑w a ~1/n times #w? If w normalized sum1, g ~1/n * some ~1e-4. Unless scale? w over lags sum1, each a~1/n, g~1/n. η should be large (n) to effect. Previous uses `gmax` scaling. We can scale `g = g / max(g)` so values O(1), η range 0.01-1. Good. But then dimensionless; okay.
  However gradient of J with weights normalized has scale 1/n; scaling arbitrary for step. Use `g/gmax`.
- Try eta list [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0] maybe with line search. Multiplicative can reduce total; normalize.

- `soft_descent(a, time_limit, tau_list...)`:
   - Initialize tau maybe `max(0.05*M)`, decrease when improving, increase when stuck.
   - Each iteration: compute c, v=n*c, Mv. If improved best update.
   - Weights with tau.
   - Direction g.
   - line search eta list (maybe adaptive).
   - If no accept, tau*=1.5, maybe smooth/restart.
   - Use counter for stagnation.

- `lbfgs_refine(a, time_limit, tau)`: Use scipy minimize on x=log(a) with soft objective.
   Implementation details:
   `def _lbfgs_obj(x):`
       top-level can't nest. We'll define a function `_lbfgs_function(x, L, m, n, tau)` returning value, grad.
       - `u = x.copy(); u[-1]=0; u -= np.max(u)` for stability.
       - `p = np.exp(u); s = p.sum(); a = p/s`.
       - conv = fft conv.
       - v = n*c.
       - Mv = max(v). soft = tau*(Mv + log(sum(exp((v-Mv)/tau)))) (with clip).
       - weights w = exp((v-Mv)/tau) normalized.
       - `F = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L); W = np.fft.rfft(w, L); g = irfft(W*F)[n-1:m]` (∂J/∂a without 2? J=soft; derivative 2∑w a shifted. Our g maybe sum; include 2`.
       - grad_a = 2*g (since J soft max weights already? derivative of soft max =∑w c_k. ∂c_k/∂a =2 shifted. So 2g.)
       - Convert to x: grad_x = a * (grad_a - dot(grad_a, a)); set grad_x[-1]=0.
       - Return float(soft), grad_x.
   - Initial x = log(a0), set [-1]? Use `x = np.log(a0); x -= x[-1]`. To avoid gauge, set last 0.
   - `res = minimize(_lbfgs_function, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': 1000, 'maxfun': 20000, 'maxls': 50, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'disp':False})`.
   - During minimize, we can't time-limit; set maxfun. After, compute exact internal score. If fails, use best from res? L-BFGS may produce x with overflow? We'll clip.
   - Run multiple tau values: [0.05,0.02,0.005,0.001] if time. Use last result as input for next tau. Stop when time.

   Potential issue: The objective soft with tau=0.001 and v values ~1.5, gradient weights extremely concentrated; L-BFGS might fail due to near nondifferentiable. But okay.

- `lp_direction(a, L, m, n, K, bound)`:
   - conv, v, Mv. Active indices: top K (or threshold). We can choose `active = np.where(v > Mv - max(0.05, 0.02*Mv))[0]`, cap K=150 by top. If len too large, sort.
   - Build A = np.zeros((K, n+1)). For each row r,k: valid i range max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k). coeff =2*n*a[valid] (derivative of v_k = n c_k). Since v=n c. Then A[r, valid]=coeff. A[r,n]=1. b zeros.
   - lo = -bound*a; hi=bound*a; t bounds (-10,10). `res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(K), A_eq=np.ones(n), b_eq=0, bounds=list(zip(lo,hi))+[(-10,10)], method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-6})`. However A_eq sum δ=0. The t variable not in equality. c = [0]*n + [-1].
   - Because constraints `coeff*δ + t <=0`, maximizing t means t = -min coeff δ. If all coeff δ can be negative, t positive. But if coefficients all positive (a nonnegative), to make coeff δ negative, δ must be negative where a_{k-i} large, and positive elsewhere to sum zero. Feasible.
   - We can scale A rows by maybe no.
   - If res.status==0, t=res.fun? Since c=-1, res.fun = -t. So t_opt=-res.fun. If t_opt>1e-5, delta=res.x[:n]. Else no.
   - To avoid many t variables unbounded negative? bounds t (-10,10).
   - Time: `linprog` dense. We can implement a fallback block LP.

Block LP:
   - block size b=8 or 16. nblocks = ceil(n/b). For each block, total mass `mk`. Variables δ_block constant values y_b? To maintain sum: ∑ b*y_b=0 => ∑ y_b? If block sizes equal except last, use `weights wblock = block lengths`; equality `Σ wblock_j * y_j =0`. Bounds y_j ∈ [-bound*mk_j / wblock_j? Actually if δ constant y in block, total δ block = len*y. Nonneg per element: if y negative, need -y ≤ min_a in block. To be safe, use y bound = bound * min_a_block (very small) or bound*mean but ensure nonneg by line search? If y negative and min small, may make negative; line search can limit but easier bound = bound * min_a_block (could be zero if a_i tiny).    - Alternative use variables for block total η_j, with constraints η_j ≤ len_j * bound * min? Hmm.
   - Could skip block LP, use full LP if time/size. For n=8192 and K=80, dense A 80x8193 ~655k floats (5MB), fine. HiGHS maybe okay. We can limit LP calls (e.g., every 20 soft iterations). For n=16384, 80x16385 ~1.3M (10MB), okay. We'll use full LP with K=50-100 and maybe early exit if time.

   To speed LP, we can use only active variables with a_i > threshold? But constraints need all. Could use `scipy.optimize.linprog` with `method='highs'` and `A_ub` as sparse? HiGHS accepts sparse matrices. Use `scipy.sparse.csr_matrix`. Build row by row. That reduces memory but maybe overhead. Dense okay. We can build dense as list of rows using slicing.

```
A = np.zeros((K, n+1))
for r,k in enumerate(active):
   lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k)
   A[r, lo:hi] = 2*n*a[k-hi+1:k? wait index]
```
We need coeff for δ_i: a_{k-i}, with i from 0..n-1. Valid i: 0≤i<n,0≤k-i<n -> k-n+1 ≤ i ≤ k. So slice i_start=max(0,k-n+1), i_end=min(n,k) exclusive. For i in slice, index in a = k-i. When i increases, k-i decreases from k-i_start to k-(i_end-1). Slicing `a[k - i : k - i_start - 1 : -1]` messy. We can compute valid i array and assign `2*n*a[k - i_slice]`. Simpler create `idx = np.arange(i_start, i_end); A[r, idx] = 2*n*a[k - idx]`. For K=100,n=8000, 800k operations okay. But allocating idx each row. Or use `np.concatenate`? We'll do idx.
   - `A[r, n]=1`.
   - bounds: list of tuples length n+1; constructing each LP call can be costly. We can precompute? a changes. Use `bounds = [(-bound*a_i, bound*a_i) for a_i in a] + [(-10,10)]` maybe slow for 8000 but okay. Better use `np.array`? `linprog` bounds accept list of (lo,hi). Could pass `bounds=(lo, hi)`? SciPy linprog accepts bounds as tuple (lb, ub) arrays? Yes `bounds` can be (lb, ub) arrays or list. Use `bounds=(-bound*a, 10?)`? For t upper, need arrays. `linprog(c, ..., bounds=(-bound*a_test, bound*a_test))` where last entries include t. `lb = -bound*a; lb[-1]=-10; ub=bound*a; ub[-1]=10`. This is accepted? I think bounds can be an array_like of shape (2, N) or tuple (lb,ub). Yes.
   - `A_eq = np.ones(n+1); A_eq[-1]=0; b_eq=0`.
   - `c = np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=-1`.
   - `res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(K), A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=0, bounds=(lb,ub), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-6, 'dual_infeasibility_tolerance':1e-6, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8, 'time_limit': time_left?})`.    If LP fails, fallback to multiplicative direction.

- `smooth_filter(a, width)`: use `np.convolve(a, np.ones(w)/w, mode='same')` or FFT low-pass. To avoid edges overshoot, use uniform. After smoothing, normalize. - `resize_sequence(a, n_target)`: Use `np.interp` on cumulative? For nonnegative unsorted? Sequence order matters, interpolation works. To preserve sum, normalized. - `generate_priors(n, rng)`: Return list of normalized arrays.
   - If height_sequence_1 global exists, resize to n and include.
   - Shape candidates:
      * Linear increasing/decreasing with min small: p_i=(1+κ*(2t-1)) normalized for κ in [0.2,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0] and negative. But many have peaks; still.
      * Power-law asymmetric: p_i=(i+1)^α (n-i)^β for α,β grid [-0.5,0,0.5,1,2,3] maybe. Actually zero endpoints if α>0. Use epsilon=0.5. Normalize.
      * Low-degree Fourier: p_i=exp(κ0+κ1 cos 2πt+κ2 sin+κ3 cos2π+κ4 sin+ linear). Random coefficients or grid.
      * Random smooth: Fourier coefficients decaying: x=0; for k=1..K amp = sqrt(1/k) random phase; add random monotone trend; exp normalize.
      * Two/three Gaussian bumps with centers random, widths; nonnegative. This can create anti-correlation.
   - Use only a handful (e.g., 30) to not waste.

Let's define an internal search function `_search_one(n, budget, rng, initial=None)` returning best normalized array and internal score. It uses the components and time.

`_search_one`:
- Setup L, m.
- Initialize candidate list: parent resized, shape priors (if no parent maybe). Keep top K by score.
- Set `a` best initial, `best_score`.
- Main loop until time:
   - Alternate methods: soft descent for some time, LP step, L-BFGS refine.
   - For each improvement update best.
   - Stagnation triggers reshaping/prior.
- Return best_a, score.

Detailed loop:
```

   method_choice based on iteration/time:
      - First 30%: soft descent
      - Then every 50 soft iters: LP
      - Every 300s: L-BFGS for 60s
   timeout for each method: e.g., soft 60s, LP 2s per call, L-BFGS 120s.
```

Let's define `_soft_descent`:
```
def _soft_descent(a, L, m, n, rng, time_limit, tau_start=0.2, tau_min=0.002):
   if time_limit<=0: return a, score
   deadline=...
   a=normalize(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
   tau=tau_start
   eta=0.2
   stall=0
   it=0
   while now<deadline:
      c=fft_conv(a); v=n*c; Mv=v.max(); sc=Mv
      if sc<best_score-1e-12: update
      w, _ = soft_weights(v, tau)
      g=gradient_dir(a,w)
      gmax=g.max(); if gmax==0 break; g/=gmax
      improved=False
      for e in [eta, 0.5*eta, 2*eta?]:
          a2=a*np.exp(-e*g); a2=normalize(a2); sc2=score
          if sc2<sc-1e-13: a=a2; improved; break
      if improved:
          eta=min(1.5, eta*1.1)
          tau=max(tau_min, tau*0.97)
          stall=0
      else:
          eta=max(0.02, eta*0.7)
              stall+=1
          if stall>30: tau=min(1.0, tau*1.8)
          if stall>80: a=start from best + random mix; etc.
      it+=1
   return best_a,best_score
```
Need maybe try positive direction? Multiplicative `exp(-e*g)` always reduces high derivative positions. Could also try `exp(+e*g)`? Usually not. We can do both if no accept? Maybe gradient points to increase active; negative reduces, positive increases. So negative.

Weights `w` should sum 1. Using `z=(v-Mv)/tau`, max z=0. If tau too small, only max lags weight. Good.

Potential issue: `g` normalized by max loses scale; step eta in dimensionless. Values a_i multiplied by exp(-eta*gmax). If gmax corresponds to high derivative locations. Good.

Add a small uniform baseline to w to avoid zero? Soft weights positive for all but underflow. We can clip z at -50, so baseline ~1e-22; fine.

Iterative LP interplay: LP step may produce non-smooth a. After LP, we can smooth slightly if improves.

Let's think about using the LP step frequently. The active LP uses top K lags. Since objective v^* max, K maybe 32. The linearized LP direction might be strong. We'll integrate into main loop:
- Run soft descent for `soft_time` (e.g., 120s).
- Then run LP step repeatedly for, say, 30s: each LP call build and solve; if improve, update; if no improve for some calls, break.
- Run L-BFGS for 60-120s with small tau.
- Repeat.

Or interleave: every 20 soft iterations, one LP. Soft descent internal loop can call LP on stagnation. Simpler: main loop of methods with time allocations:
```
phase=0

  if phase 0: soft 120s
  elif phase 1: lp 3 calls
  elif phase 2: lbfgs 120s
  elif phase 3: soft small tau 120s
  ... phase cycles. On stagnation, reset phase and maybe prior.
```
Ensure total.

Let's outline code with top-level functions:
```
import math, time, numpy as np
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY=True
except: HAVE_SCIPY=False
```
No lambdas.

Helper functions:
- `_safe_float_budget`
- `_normalize_array(a)` (returns copy normalized)
- `_fft_len(n)`: 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
- `_self_conv(a, L, m)`: uses rfft.
- `_score_normalized(c,n)`: float(2*n*np.max(c))
- `_score_seq_normalized(a)`: maybe sum check.
- `_soft_weights(v, tau)`: if tau<=0 uniform top 1; else z=(v-max)/tau; clip; exp; normalize.
- `_gradient_for_weights(a,w,L,m,n)`: b=a[::-1]; F=rfft(b,L); W=rfft(w,L); g=irfft(W*F,L)[n-1:m]; np.maximum(g,0,out=g); return g
- `_soft_descent(...)`
- `_lbfgs_objective(x, L, m, n, tau)`
- `_lbfgs_refine(...)`
- `_build_lp_matrix(a, active, n)`: scale=2*n; b=a[::-1]; A=np.zeros((len(active), n+1)); for each ...
- `_lp_step_once(a, K, bound, n, L, m)`: returns a2 or None.
- `_lp_refine(...)`
- `_smooth_array(a, w)`
- `_random_prior(n, rng)`
- `_parametric_priors(n, rng)`
- `_resize_sequence(a, n)`
- `_eval_internal(seq, n?)` maybe.
- `_internal_exact(seq)`: FFT or np.convolve.
- `_exact_score(seq)`: try evaluate_sequence.
- `_search_one(n, budget, rng, init_arrays)`: main.
- `propose_candidate...`

Let's detail `_soft_descent` to avoid infinite if no direction:
```
def _soft_descent(a, L, m, n, rng, time_limit, tau_start=0.15, tau_min=0.003):
    if time_limit <= 0: return a, _score...
    deadline = time.monotonic()+time_limit
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c=_self_conv(...); best_score=_score_norm(c,n); best_a=a.copy()
    tau=tau_start; eta=0.1; stall=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        c=_self_conv(a,...); v=n*c; Mv=float(np.max(v))
        if Mv < best_score - 1e-12: best...
        w=_soft_weights(v, tau)
        g=_gradient_for_weights(a,w,L,m,n)
        gmax=float(np.max(g))
        if gmax <= 0: break
        g /= gmax
        improved=False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0): # eta*factor
             step=eta*factor; if step<=0: continue
             a2=a*np.exp(-step*g)
             a2=_normalize_array(a2)
             c2=_self_conv(a2); sc2=float(2*n*np.max(c2))
             if sc2 < Mv - 1e-13:
                 a=a2; Mv=sc2; improved=True; break
        if improved:
             if Mv < best_score -1e-12: best...
             eta = min(2.0, eta*1.12)
             tau = max(tau_min, tau*0.97)
             stall=0
        else:
             eta = max(0.02, eta*0.75)
             stall +=1
             if stall > 25:
                 tau = min(1.0, tau*1.7)
             if stall > 80:
                 # mix with best and random
                 mix = 0.1 + 0.2*rng.random()

    return best_a,best_score

   L,m=...

   inits = list(init_arrays)
   inits.extend(_parametric_priors(n,rng, score=False)) maybe score later.

   a = best; best_score.
   pool = top arrays
   pool_idx=0
   methods cycle. Use `stall_count` best improvements.
   last_best_time=now
   while now < deadline:
      remaining=deadline-now
      if remaining < 20: break
      # if no improvement for 120s, refresh a from pool or random
      if now-last_best_time > 120:
          if pool_idx < len(pool): a=pool[pool_idx].copy(); pool_idx+=1
          else: a=normalize(0.8*best+0.2*random)
          # score a; if best maybe update
          last_best_time=now
      if method step soft:
          a,sc=_soft_descent(a, ..., min(90, remaining))
      elif lp:
          a,sc=_lp_refine(a, min(30, remaining), ...)
      elif lbfgs:
          a,sc=_lbfgs_refine(a, min(90, remaining), ...)
      if sc<best_score: best_a=a; best_score=sc; last_best_time=now; update pool
      method cycle.
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _lp_refine(a, time_limit, K_start=40, K_max=160):
   deadline=...
   best_a=a; best_score=score
   no_improve=0
   K=K_start
   while now < deadline-3:
       res = _lp_step_once(a, K, bound=0.3)
       if res is not None and score<best_score:
            a=res; no_improve=0; K=min(K_max, int(K*1.2))
       else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve >=3:
                if K < K_max: K=min(K_max, K*2)
                else: break
   return best_a,best_score
```

t = np.linspace(0,1,n)
eps = max(1.0/(4*n), 1e-3)
logt = np.log(t+eps); logq=np.log(1-t+eps)
out=[]
alphas = [-0.4,-0.25,-0.1,0,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5]
betas = [-0.4,-0.25,-0.1,0,0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5]
for a in alphas:
 for b in betas:
   lp = a*logt + b*logq
   # maybe omit symmetric high? keep all
   p = np.exp(lp - lp.max()); _normalize_array
   out.append(p)
# addrandom Fourier
for _ in range(8): out.append(_random_prior(n,rng))
# add step priors
for a in [0.3,0.5,0.7]:
 for d in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
   p = 1.0 + d*(t<a - (t>=a)) # values 1±d; ensure nonneg; if d<1 ok
   p=np.max(0,p); normalize
   out.extend(p, p[::-1])
# maybe cap out to max_priors by rough score? We can return first max? But scoring later. We can return all; caller keeps top.
```

`_random_prior` details:
```
def _random_prior(n,rng):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   x=np.zeros(n)
   if n>1:
      x += rng.uniform(-1.2,1.2)*(2*t-1)
      x += rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)*np.log(t+1e-3)
      x += rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)*np.log(1-t+1e-3)
   # Fourier
   amp = rng.uniform(0,1,10)/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,11))
   for k in range(1,11):
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
       x += amp[k-1]*np.cos(2π k t + ph)
   # noise lowpass
   noise=rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
   if n>5: noise=np.convolve(noise, np.ones(5)/5, 'same')
   x += noise*0.3
   p=np.exp(x - x.max()); normalize
```
This prior may be noisy; okay for restarts.

`_build_lp_matrix(a, active, n)`:
```
K=len(active); A=np.zeros((K,n+1)); scale=2.0*n; b=a[::-1]
for r,k in enumerate(active):
    i_start = max(0, k-n+1)
    i_end = min(n, k+1)
    length = i_end - i_start
    if length >0:
        j_start = n - 1 - k + i_start
        # slice b[j_start:j_start+length]
        A[r, i_start:i_end] = scale * b[j_start:j_start+length]
    A[r, n]=1.0
return A
```
Check j_start+length ≤ n: if k<n, j_start=n-1-k, len=k+1 -> end n. if k≥n, j_start=0, len=n -> end n. Good.

       sc=score(a)
       if sc < best_score: update
       last_best=now
       it_type=0
   # optimize a for one slice

   elif it_type==1: a,sc=_lp_refine(a, time=min(30, rem*0.12))
   elif it_type==2: a,sc=_lbfgs_refine(a, time=min(120, rem*0.3), tau=0.02)
   elif it_type==3:
       # smooth then soft low tau
       a,sc=_smooth_try(a,L,m,n)
       a,sc=_soft_descent(a, tau_start=0.02, tau_min=0.002, time=min(90, rem*0.25))
   else: it_type=0; stall? 
   it_type=(it_type+1)%4
   if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
       best_a=a.copy(); best_score=sc; last_best=now
       # add mutated best to pending? maybe if score improved, keep optimizing same
   elif it_type==0 and now-last_best > 90:
       # force restart sooner
```

def _lbfgs_refine(a, L, m, n, time_limit, tau=0.03):
   if time_limit <20 or not HAVE_MINIMIZE:
       return a, score
   x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   # center x0 to avoid large constant
   x0 = x0 - np.mean(x0)
   last_best_a=a; last_best_score=score
   best_a=...; best_score...
   # run maybe multiple taus
   taus = [max(0.08, tau*2), tau, max(0.005, tau*0.33)]
   for t in taus:
       if now >= deadline-10: break
       rem=...
       maxfun = int(max(300, rem*40))
       try:
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_objective, x0, args=(L,m,n,t), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':300,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-7,'disp':False})
          xf = res.x
          u=xf - xf.max(); p=np.exp(u); a2=p/p.sum(); a2=_normalize_array(a2)
          c=_self_conv(a2,...); sc=...; if sc<best_score: best...
          # set x0 from new best? If improved, x0=xf; else keep x0 from best? Need x0 representing best? If not, use best a log for next tau.
          if sc < last_best_score: x0=xf? But xf may not correspond to best? It is result. Use if sc<last_best_score.
       except Exception: pass
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0)
        return a / float(a.size)
    a /= s
    # fix tiny negatives
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s2=float(a.sum())
    if s2>0: a/=s2
    return a
```

`_fft_len(n)`:
```
m=2*n-1
return 1 << (m-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 1
```
If m=1, L=1. Use `L=1; while L<m: L*=2` but bit_length faster. For m=3, (m-1).bit_length(): m=3 ->2.bit_length=2, 1<<2=4. Good. For m=1 ->0.bit_length=0, 1.

`_self_conv(a,L,m)`:
```
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:m]
c=np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64) # ascontiguousarray doesn't have dtype? Use np.array(..., copy=False)
np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
return c
```
`np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)` works? It accepts dtype. Good.

`_score_c(c,n)`:
```
if c.size==0: return 0
return 2.0*float(n)*float(np.max(c))
```

`_soft_weights(v, tau)`:
```
m=v.size; w=np.zeros(m)
mval=float(np.max(v))
if tau <= 1e-12:
   idx=np.where(v >= mval-1e-12)[0]; if len==0 idx=[argmax]; w[idx]=1/len; return w
z=(v-mval)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-50,0); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if not finite...; w=e/s; return w
```

`_gradient_for_weights(a,w,L,m,n)`:
```
b=a[::-1].copy()
F=np.fft.rfft(b, L)
W=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
g=np.fft.irfft(W*F, L)[n-1:m]
g=np.ascontiguousarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
return g
```
Need `n-1:m` length n? m=2n-1, n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. Yes.

`_soft_descent` as above. In line search, `a2 = a * np.exp(-step*g)`. If step*g large negative, exp underflow; okay. Normalize. Use `for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.75):`. If g all zeros break.

When updating best inside soft, update `best_score` and `best_a`. At restart, use `prior = _random_prior(n, r`ng)`; `mix=0.2+...`; `a = _normalize_array((1-mix)*best_a + mix*prior)`. Reset `step` etc. Use local `score=...` after restart.

`_lbfgs_objective` must not be too slow. It uses `_self_conv` and `_gradient_for_weights`.

```
def _lbfgs_objective(x, L, m, n, tau):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
    u = x - float(np.max(x))
    p = np.exp(u)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    a = p/s
    c = _self_conv(a,L,m)
    score_vals = (2.0*float(n))*c
    mx = float(np.max(score_vals))
    z = (score_vals - mx)/tau
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
    val = tau*(mx + math.log(se))
    w = e/se
    g = _gradient_for_weights(a,w,L,m,n)
    grad_a = (2.0*float(n))*g
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
    return val, grad_x
```
Since `score_vals` is 2n c, gradient correct. `score_vals` may have tiny negative; max okay. `e` sum maybe. Good.

LP functions: `_lp_step_once` could be slow due to `linprog` if A_ub large. We'll limit K in calls. Use if not HAVE_SCIPY return None.
```
def _lp_step_once(a,K,bound,L,m,n):
   c=_self_conv(a,L,m); v=2.0*n*c; order=np.argsort(v)[-min(K, m):]
   active=order[::-1].astype(int)
   A=np.zeros((len(active), n+1), dtype=np.float64)
   scale=2.0*float(n); b=a[::-1]
   for r,k in enumerate(active):
      start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
      length=end-start
      if length>0:
          jstart=n-1-k+start
          A[r,start:end] = scale * b[jstart:jstart+length]
      A[r,n]=1.0
   cvec=np.zeros(n+1); cvec[-1]=-1.0
   lb=-bound*a.copy(); ub=bound*a.copy(); lb=np.concatenate([lb, np.array([-10.0])]); ub=...
   Aeq=np.zeros(n+1); Aeq[:n]=1.0; beq=0.0
   b_ub=np.zeros(len(active))
   try:
       if HAVE_LINPROG:
          try: res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=beq,bounds=(lb,ub),method='highs',options={'presolve':True})
          except TypeError: res=linprog(...)
       else: return None
   except Exception: return None
   if res.status != 0 or not hasattr(res,'x'): return None
   delta=res.x[:n]; delta=np.where(np.isfinite(delta),delta,0)
   base=2.0*n*float(np.max(c))
   best_a=None; best_sc=base
   neg=delta<0
   lam_max=1.0
   if np.any(neg):
       vals=a[neg]/(-delta[neg])
       finite=vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
       if finite.size: lam_max=min(lam_max, float(np.min(finite)))
   if lam_max <= 1e-6: return None
   scorso=lam_max
   for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0):
       lam=frac*lam_max
       if lam<=1e-8: continue
       a2=a+lam*delta
       # if negatives due to rounding clip
       np.maximum(a2,0.0,out=a2)
       a2=_normalize_array(a2)
       c2=_self_conv(a2,L,m); sc2=2.0*n*float(np.max(c2))
       if sc2 < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc2; best_a=a2
   if best_a is not None and best_sc < base - 1e-12:
       return best_a
   return None
```
Note `np.maximum(a2,0,out=a2)` before `_normalize_array` which also clips. Good.

Let's code propose carefully.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except Exception: budget=2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 60: budget=max(60, float(budget) if np.isfinite(budget) else 2400.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       h = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if h is not None:
          parent=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          if parent.size>0:
             parent=np.where(np.isfinite(parent),parent,0.0)
             parent=np.maximum(parent,0.0)
    except Exception: parent=None
    reserve_exact = 45.0
    search_end = start + max(30.0, budget - reserve_exact)
    candidates=[]
    small_a=None
    if budget > 1200 and search_end - start > 250:
       n_small = 1024
       t_small = min(120.0, 0.08*(budget-reserve_exact))
       inits=[]
       if parent is not None: inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent,n_small))
       small_a, small_sc = _search_one(n_small, t_small, rng, inits)
       candidates.append((small_sc,n_small,small_a))
    # main
    if budget > 800: n_main=8192
    elif budget > 400: n_main=4096
    else: n_main=2048
    high_time=0.0
    high_n=0
    if budget > 1800 and search_end - start > 800:
       high_n=12288
       high_time = min(300.0, 0.18*(budget-reserve_exact))
    t_main_limit = max(60.0, search_end - start - high_time - 30.0)
    main_inits=[]
    if small_a is not None: main_inits.append(_resize_sequence(small_a,n_main))
    if parent is not None: main_inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent,n_main))
    t_main = min(t_main_limit, search_end - start)
    main_a, main_sc = _search_one(n_main, t_main, rng, main_inits)
    candidates.append((main_sc,n_main,main_a))
    if high_n > 0 and high_time > 60:
       high_inits=[_resize_sequence(main_a, high_n)]
       if parent is not None: high_inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent,high_n))
       high_a, high_sc = _search_one(high_n, high_time, rng, high_inits)
       candidates.append((high_sc,high_n,high_a))
    # extra refinement if time
    rem = search_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90 and candidates:
       # find best internal
       best_item = candidates[0]
       for item in candidates[1:]:
          if item[0] < best_item[0]: best_item=item
       target_n = max(n_main, high_n if high_n else 0)
       if best_item[1] < target_n:
           init=[_resize_sequence(best_item[2], target_n)] + ([_resize_sequence(parent,target_n)] if parent is not None else [])
       else:
           init=[best_item[2]] + ([_resize_sequence(parent,best_item[1])] if parent is not None else [])
       arr,sc=_search_one(target_n, rem-30, rng, init)
       candidates.append((sc,target_n,arr))
    # final exact
    hard_end = start + budget - 3.0
    ranked=[]
    for i,item in enumerate(candidates):
       ranked.append((item[0], i))
    ranked.sort()
    best_exact = np.inf; best_seq=None
    # safe lower n eval: best item with n<=9000
    safe_idx=-1; safe_score=np.inf
    for i,item in enumerate(candidates):
       if item[1] <= 9000 and item[0] < safe_score:
           safe_score=item[0]; safe_idx=i
    if safe_idx >=0 and time.monotonic() < hard_end - 20:
       seq=[float(max(0.0, x)) for x in candidates[safe_idx][2]]
       val=_exact_score(seq)
       if np.isfinite(val): best_exact=val; best_seq=seq
    # evaluate top by internal
    count=1 if safe_idx>=0 and best_seq is not None else 0
    if count==0: # maybe safe exact failed; count still 0
       pass
    for score, idx in ranked:
       if time.monotonic() > hard_end - 10: break
       if count >= 4: break
       if idx == safe_idx: continue
       n = candidates[idx][1]
       if n > 16500: continue
       # avoid evaluating if internal much worse than current best_exact? if best_exact finite and score > best_exact +0.02 maybe skip? But internal score vs exact comparable.
       if best_exact < np.inf and score > best_exact + 0.05: continue
       arr=candidates[idx][2]
       seq=[float(max(0.0, x)) for x in arr]
       val=_exact_score(seq)
       count +=1
       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
          best_exact=val; best_seq=seq
    if best_seq is None:
       # choose best internal
       best_item=candidates[0]
       for item in candidates[1:]:
          if item[0] < best_item[0]: best_item=item
       arr=best_item[2]
       arr=_normalize_array(arr)
       best_seq=[float(max(0.0, x)) for x in arr]
    # If all values zero? should not.
    return best_seq
```

Let's think about exact score for high n if `evaluate_sequence` uses `np.convolve` on a list and may be O(n^2) memory. 12288 convolve result length 24575, memory small. Direct convolution in numpy for 1D? It may use direct C loops; okay.

Now, possible hidden time: `propose_candidate` itself has 2400s; our exact reserve 45s. If `_search_one` overruns due to `linprog`/minimize ignoring deadline, total may exceed. `minimize` maxfun limited but not time; if each eval slow, could exceed. We set maxfun based on time with factor 15; if eval 0.1s and time 100s, maxfun=1500 =>150s over. Use more conservative factor 5? For n=8192, eval maybe 0.05s? Let's estimate: FFT 8192 in numpy ~0.0002s? Python function overhead plus gradient maybe 0.001-0.005. 5*rem maybe okay. For n=12288 maybe 0.005. Use `maxfun = int(max(300, min(3000, rem * 10)))`. If rem=100, 1000 evals; if eval .01s =>10s, safe; if .1s=>100s over. But unlikely. We can also run `minimize` with `disp=False`; no time callback. Accept.

LP `linprog` could be slow. K start32, A 32x8193 ~0.26M. HiGHS maybe 0.1s. For n=12288 K up to128 ~1.5M, maybe 1s. A few calls within 45s. Good.

Now, code size perhaps large but okay.

Let's think if any function uses `np.maximum(c,0,out=c)` on non-writable arrays from `irfft`? `irfft` returns writable. Good.

```python
import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _normalize_array(a):
    """Return a nonnegative float array whose entries sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a.fill(1.0)
        return a / float(a.size)
    a /= s
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s2 = float(a.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        a /= s2
    return a


def _fft_len(n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _self_conv(a, L, m):
    """Exact (FFT) linear self-convolution of a, zero-padded to length L."""
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:m]
    c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_c(c, n):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(c))


def _score_a(a, L, m, n):
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    return _score_c(c, n)


def _soft_weights(v, tau):
    """Approximate subgradient of max(v) by a softmax over v."""
    m = v.size
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return w
    mx = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    if tau <= 1e-12:
        idx = np.where(v >= mx - 1e-12)
        if idx[0].size == 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[idx[0]] = 1.0 / float(idx[0].size)
        return w
    z = (v - mx) / float(tau)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def _gradient_for_weights(a, w, L, m, n):
    """
    For J = sum_k w_k * conv(a,a)_k, return dJ/da up to a factor.
    Specifically returns sum_k w_k a_{k-i}.
    """
    b = a[::-1].copy()
    F = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    g = np.fft.irfft(W * F, L)[n - 1:m]
    g = np.ascontiguousarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def _soft_descent(a, L, m, n, rng, time_limit, tau_start=0.10, tau_min=0.002):
    """
    Multiplicative soft-max descent for 2*n*max(self-conv).
    """
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize_array(a)
        return a, _score_a(a, L, m, n)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()

    tau = float(tau_start)
    step = 0.20
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
        score = _score_c(c, n)
        v = float(n) * c

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()

        w = _soft_weights(v, tau)
        g = _gradient_for_weights(a, w, L, m, n)
        gmax = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
            break
        g /= gmax

        accepted = False
        chosen = step
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 4.0, 0.75):
            st = step * factor
            if st <= 1e-12:
                continue
            a2 = a * np.exp(-st * g)
            a2 = _normalize_array(a2)
            c2 = _self_conv(a2, L, m)
            sc2 = _score_c(c2, n)
            if sc2 < score - 1e-14:
                a = a2
                score = sc2
                accepted = True
                chosen = st
                break

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            if chosen == step or chosen > step:
                step = min(1.5, step * 1.13)
            else:
                step = max(0.03, step * 0.90)
            tau = max(tau_min, tau * 0.965)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.72)
            if no_accept > 25:
                tau = min(1.0, tau * 1.7)
            if no_accept > 70:
                prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
                mix = 0.15 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())
                a = _normalize_array((1.0 - mix) * best_a + mix * prior)
                c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
                score = _score_c(c, n)
                if score < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                tau = max(tau_min, min(float(tau_start), tau * 0.35))
                step = 0.20
                no_accept = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            tau = min(0.5, tau * 1.2)

    return best_a, best_score


def _lbfgs_objective(x, L, m, n, tau):
    """
    L-BFGS objective on log-densities x.
    a = softmax(x).  Objective is a smoothed version of 2*n*max(self-conv).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
    u = x - float(np.max(x))
    p = np.exp(u)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    a = p / s
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    val_vec = (2.0 * float(n)) * c
    mx = float(np.max(val_vec))
    z = (val_vec - mx) / float(tau)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    val = float(tau) * (mx + math.log(se))
    w = e / se

    g = _gradient_for_weights(a, w, L, m, n)
    grad_a = (2.0 * float(n)) * g
    dot = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
    return val, grad_x


def _lbfgs_refine(a, L, m, n, time_limit, tau=0.03):
    """
    Use L-BFGS-B on softmax parameters to reduce a smooth maximum.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        a = _normalize_array(a)
        return a, _score_a(a, L, m, n)

    if time_limit is None or time_limit < 30.0:
        a = _normalize_array(a)
        return a, _score_a(a, L, m, n)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()

    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))

    taus = (max(0.08, 2.0 * tau), max(0.025, tau), max(0.006, 0.35 * tau))
    for t in taus:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        maxfun = int(max(300, min(3500, 10.0 * rem)))
        options = {
            "maxiter": 300,
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxls": 50,
            "ftol": 1e-14,
            "gtol": 1e-7,
            "disp": False,
        }
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lbfgs_objective,
                x0,
                args=(L, m, n, float(t)),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options=options,
            )
            xf = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            xf = np.clip(xf, -700.0, 700.0)
            u = xf - float(np.max(xf))
            p = np.exp(u)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            a2 = np.maximum(p / s, 0.0)
            a2 = _normalize_array(a2)
            sc2 = _score_a(a2, L, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                x0 = xf.copy()
                x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
            else:
                x0 = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
                x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
        except Exception:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def _lp_step_once(a, K, bound, L, m, n):
    """
    One linearized active-set step.

    For current a, active lags are those with largest 2*n*conv.  Solve an LP
    for an additive perturbation delta that first-order decreases those lags.
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None

    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    val_vec = (2.0 * float(n)) * c
    if c.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(float(np.max(val_vec))):
        return None

    K = int(max(1, min(K, c.size)))
    order = np.argsort(val_vec)[-K:]
    active = order[::-1].astype(int, copy=False)

    A = np.zeros((active.size, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    scale = 2.0 * float(n)
    b = a[::-1]

    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(n, k + 1)
        length = i1 - i0
        if length > 0:
            j0 = n - 1 - k + i0
            A[r, i0:i1] = scale * b[j0:j0 + length]
        A[r, n] = 1.0

    cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cvec[-1] = -1.0

    lb = -float(bound) * a.copy()
    ub = float(bound) * a.copy()
    lb = np.concatenate((lb, np.array([-10.0], dtype=np.float64)))
    ub = np.concatenate((ub, np.array([10.0], dtype=np.float64)))

    Aeq = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[:n] = 1.0
    beq = 0.0
    bub = np.zeros(active.size, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                A_eq=Aeq,
                b_eq=beq,
                bounds=(lb, ub),
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bub,
                A_eq=Aeq,
                b_eq=beq,
                bounds=(lb, ub),
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0 or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
        return None

    delta = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    delta = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, 0.0)

    base = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a2 = None
    best_score = base

    lam_max = 1.0
    neg = delta < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        vals = a[neg] / (-delta[neg])
        vals = vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
        if vals.size:
            lam_max = min(lam_max, float(np.min(vals)))

    if lam_max <= 1e-7:
        return None

    for frac in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 0.85, 1.00):
        lam = frac * lam_max
        if lam <= 1e-9:
            continue
        a2 = a + lam * delta
        np.maximum(a2, 0.0, out=a2)
        a2 = _normalize_array(a2)
        c2 = _self_conv(a2, L, m)
        sc2 = _score_c(c2, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a2 = a2

    if best_a2 is not None and best_score < base - 1e-12:
        return best_a2
    return None


def _lp_refine(a, L, m, n, time_limit, K_start=32, K_max=128, bound=0.45):
    """Repeated LP active-set line searches."""
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()

    if time_limit is None or time_limit < 8.0 or not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return best_a, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    K = int(K_start)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
        r = _lp_step_once(a, K, bound, L, m, n)
        if r is not None:
            a = _normalize_array(r)
            c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
            sc = _score_c(c, n)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                K = min(int(K_max), max(K + 8, int(1.25 * K)))
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 3:
            if K < int(K_max):
                K = min(int(K_max), max(K + 16, 2 * K))
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                break

    return best_a, best_score


def _smooth_try(a, L, m, n, widths=(3, 5, 7, 11)):
    """Accept a mild boxcar smoothing only if it improves the internal score."""
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    c = _self_conv(a, L, m)
    best_score = _score_c(c, n)
    best_a = a.copy()

    for w in widths:
        w = int(w)
        if w <= 0:
            continue
        if w >= n:
            w = n if (n % 2 == 1) else n - 1
        if w <= 1:
            continue
        if w % 2 == 0:
            w += 1
        if w > n:
            continue
        kernel = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
        b = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode="same")
        b = _normalize_array(b)
        sc = _score_a(b, L, m, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = b.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _random_prior(n, rng):
    """Smooth random asymmetric prior."""
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    x += float(rng.uniform(-1.4, 1.4)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    x += float(rng.uniform(-1.8, 1.8)) * np.log(t + 1e-3)
    x += float(rng.uniform(-1.8, 1.8)) * np.log(1.0 - t + 1e-3)

    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)) / math.sqrt(float(k))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * t + ph)

    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)
    if n > 5:
        noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(5, dtype=np.float64) / 5.0, mode="same")
    x += 0.35 * noise

    p = np.exp(x - float(np.max(x)))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return _normalize_array(p)


def _resize_sequence(a, n):
    """Resize/interpolate a nonnegative sequence to length n."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(n)

    if a.size == n:
        return _normalize_array(a)

    if a.size == 0:
        return _normalize_array(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize_array(y)


def _power_grid_priors(n, L, m, budget):
    """
    A small global search over asymmetric beta-like profiles:
        p(t) proportional to (t+eps)^alpha * (1-t+eps)^beta.
    """
    out = []
    if n < 16 or budget < 15.0:
        return out

    grid_budget = float(min(25.0, max(5.0, 0.12 * float(budget))))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + grid_budget

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = max(1.0 / (4.0 * float(n)), 1e-3)
    logt = np.log(t + eps)
    logq = np.log(1.0 - t + eps)

    if budget > 600.0 and n <= 10000:
        num = 17
    else:
        num = 11

    alphas = np.linspace(-0.45, 2.25, num)
    betas = np.linspace(-0.45, 2.25, num)

    entries = []
    for aa in alphas:
        for bb in betas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            lp = float(aa) * logt + float(bb) * logq
            mx = float(np.max(lp))
            p = np.exp(lp - mx)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            p = p / s
            sc = _score_a(p, L, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                entries.append([sc, float(aa), float(bb), p])

    if not entries:
        return out

    keys = []
    for i, e in enumerate(entries):
        keys.append((e[0], i))
    keys.sort()

    top_entries = []
    seen = set()
    for score, idx in keys[:5]:
        key = (round(entries[idx][1], 4), round(entries[idx][2], 4))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        top_entries.append(entries[idx])

    refined = list(top_entries)

    for e in top_entries[:2]:
        sc0, aa0, bb0, _ = e
        for da in np.linspace(-0.30, 0.30, 7):
            for db in np.linspace(-0.30, 0.30, 7):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                lp = (aa0 + float(da)) * logt + (bb0 + float(db)) * logq
                mx = float(np.max(lp))
                p = np.exp(lp - mx)
                s = float(p.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                    continue
                p = p / s
                sc = _score_a(p, L, m, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < sc0 + 1e-9:
                    refined.append([sc, aa0 + float(da), bb0 + float(db), p])

    if not refined:
        return out

    keys2 = []
    for i, e in enumerate(refined):
        keys2.append((e[0], i))
    keys2.sort()

    limit = 12
    take = keys2[:limit]
    for score, idx in take:
        out.append(np.asarray(refined[idx][3], dtype=np.float64).copy())

    return out


def _extra_shape_priors(n, rng):
    """A few deterministic/random step-like and Fourier priors."""
    out = []
    if n < 16:
        return out

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for a in (0.25, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75):
        for d in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 0.95):
            p = 1.0 + d * (np.where(t < a, 1.0, -1.0))
            p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
            out.append(_normalize_array(p))
            out.append(_normalize_array(p[::-1]))

    for _ in range(4):
        out.append(_random_prior(n, rng))

    return out


def _search_one(n, budget, rng, init_arrays=None):
    """
    Main local-global search at fixed length n.
    Uses power-law priors, multiplicative soft descent, LP active-set steps,
    L-BFGS refinement, smoothing, and restarts.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    L = _fft_len(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(10.0, float(budget) - 4.0)

    arrs = []
    if init_arrays is not None:
        for a in init_arrays:
            try:
                arrs.append(_resize_sequence(a, n))
            except Exception:
                pass

    power = _power_grid_priors(n, L, m, time.monotonic() - start + float(budget))
    arrs.extend(power)

    extra_n = 6 if float(budget) > 300.0 else 3
    arrs.extend(_extra_shape_priors(n, rng)[:extra_n])

    if not arrs:
        arrs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    scored = []
    for a in arrs[:80]:
        try:
            a = _normalize_array(a)
            sc = _score_a(a, L, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, a))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not scored:
        a = _normalize_array(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        sc = _score_a(a, L, m, n)
        return a, sc

    keys = [(s, i) for i, (s, _) in enumerate(scored)]
    keys.sort()

    top_arrays = []
    for s, i in keys[:min(14, len(scored))]:
        top_arrays.append(np.asarray(scored[i][1], dtype=np.float64).copy())

    best_a = top_arrays[0].copy()
    best_score = float(keys[0][0])
    pending = top_arrays[1:]

    it_type = 0
    last_best = time.monotonic()
    restart_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

        if time.monotonic() - last_best > max(90.0, 0.10 * float(budget)):
            if pending:
                a = pending.pop().copy()
            else:
                prior = _random_prior(n, rng)
                mix = 0.15 + 0.50 * float(rng.random())
                a = _normalize_array((1.0 - mix) * best_a + mix * prior)
            sc = _score_a(a, L, m, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()
            it_type = 0
            restart_count += 1
            if restart_count % 20 == 0:
                print("restart len=%d internal=%.9f t=%.1f" % (n, best_score, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)
        else:
            if best_score < 1.0:
                a = best_a.copy()
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()

        if it_type == 0:
            sl = min(180.0, max(20.0, 0.45 * rem))
            a, sc = _soft_descent(a, L, m, n, rng, sl, tau_start=0.10, tau_min=0.002)
        elif it_type == 1:
            sl = min(50.0, max(6.0, 0.12 * rem))
            a, sc = _lp_refine(a, L, m, n, sl, K_start=32, K_max=128, bound=0.45)
        elif it_type == 2:
            sl = min(160.0, max(25.0, 0.35 * rem))
            a, sc = _lbfgs_refine(a, L, m, n, sl, tau=0.03)
        else:
            a, sc = _smooth_try(a, L, m, n)
            sl = min(130.0, max(20.0, 0.35 * rem))
            a, sc = _soft_descent(a, L, m, n, rng, sl, tau_start=0.018, tau_min=0.0015)

        it_type = (it_type + 1) % 4

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _normalize_array(a)
            last_best = time.monotonic()
            if rng.random() < 0.45 and best_score < 1.60:
                mut = _normalize_array(0.94 * best_a + 0.06 * _random_prior(n, rng))
                pending.append(mut)
                if len(pending) > 24:
                    pending = pending[-24:]

        if time.monotonic() < start + 8.0 and restart_count == 0:
            print("len=%d initial/internal=%.9f" % (n, best_score), flush=True)

    return best_a, best_score


def _internal_exact(seq):
    """
    Local exact evaluator, mimicking the supplied evaluate_sequence as closely
    as needed for fallback.
    """
    if not isinstance(seq, list) or len(seq) == 0:
        return float(np.inf)
    for x in seq:
        if isinstance(x, bool) or not isinstance(x, (int, float)):
            return float(np.inf)
        if np.isnan(x) or np.isinf(x):
            return float(np.inf)

    a = [0.0 if float(x) < 0.0 else min(1000.0, float(x)) for x in seq]
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float(np.inf)

    if n <= 20000:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        L = _fft_len(n)
        m = 2 * n - 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:m]
        c = np.ascontiguousarray(c, dtype=np.float64)

    mb = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb < 0.0:
        return float(np.inf)
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def _exact_score(seq):
    """Call the externally available evaluator if present; otherwise fallback."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)  # noqa: F821
    except NameError:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = float(np.inf)
    if val is None:
        return float(np.inf)
    try:
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float(np.inf)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
        2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)**2).

    The method combines:
      - asymmetric beta/power-law family search,
      - smooth low-frequency random priors,
      - multiplicative soft-max descent,
      - active-set LP directions,
      - L-BFGS refinement in softmax parameter space,
      - multi-scale upsampling and final exact evaluation.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 60.0:
        budget = max(60.0, float(budget) if np.isfinite(budget) else 2400.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0:
                p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
                p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                if float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
                    parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    reserve_exact = 45.0
    search_end = start + max(30.0, budget - reserve_exact)

    candidates = []

    small_a = None
    if budget > 1200.0 and search_end - start > 260.0:
        n_small = 1024
        t_small = min(110.0, 0.075 * (budget - reserve_exact))
        inits = []
        if parent is not None:
            inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent, n_small))
        small_a, small_sc = _search_one(n_small, t_small, rng, inits)
        candidates.append((small_sc, n_small, small_a))
        print("small n=%d internal=%.9f t=%.1f" % (n_small, small_sc, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    if budget > 800.0:
        n_main = 8192
    elif budget > 400.0:
        n_main = 4096
    else:
        n_main = 2048

    high_n = 0
    high_time = 0.0
    if budget > 1800.0 and search_end - start > 850.0:
        high_n = 12288
        high_time = min(280.0, 0.17 * (budget - reserve_exact))

    t_main_limit = max(60.0, search_end - start - high_time - 25.0)
    t_main = min(t_main_limit, max(30.0, search_end - start))

    main_inits = []
    if small_a is not None:
        main_inits.append(_resize_sequence(small_a, n_main))
    if parent is not None:
        main_inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent, n_main))

    main_a, main_sc = _search_one(n_main, t_main, rng, main_inits)
    candidates.append((main_sc, n_main, main_a))
    print("main n=%d internal=%.9f t=%.1f" % (n_main, main_sc, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    if high_n > 0 and high_time > 70.0:
        high_inits = [_resize_sequence(main_a, high_n)]
        if parent is not None:
            high_inits.append(_resize_sequence(parent, high_n))
        high_a, high_sc = _search_one(high_n, high_time, rng, high_inits)
        candidates.append((high_sc, high_n, high_a))
        print("high n=%d internal=%.9f t=%.1f" % (high_n, high_sc, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    rem = float(search_end - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 90.0 and candidates:
        best_item = candidates[0]
        for item in candidates[1:]:
            if item[0] < best_item[0]:
                best_item = item
        target_n = max(n_main, high_n if high_n > 0 else 0)
        if target_n <= 0:
            target_n = best_item[1]
        init = []
        if best_item[1] < target_n:
            init.append(_resize_sequence(best_item[2], target_n))
        else:
            init.append(best_item[2])
        if parent is not None:
            init.append(_resize_sequence(parent, target_n))
        arr, sc = _search_one(target_n, max(30.0, rem - 30.0), rng, init)
        candidates.append((sc, target_n, arr))
        print("extra n=%d internal=%.9f t=%.1f" % (target_n, sc, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    hard_end = start + max(10.0, budget - 3.0)
    best_exact = float(np.inf)
    best_seq = None

    # Always try a safe lower-length candidate first, if available.
    safe_idx = -1
    safe_score = float(np.inf)
    for i, item in enumerate(candidates):
        if item[1] <= 9000 and item[0] < safe_score:
            safe_score = item[0]
            safe_idx = i

    evaluated = 0
    if safe_idx >= 0 and time.monotonic() < hard_end - 22.0:
        seq = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in candidates[safe_idx][2]]
        val = _exact_score(seq)
        evaluated += 1
        if np.isfinite(val):
            best_exact = val
            best_seq = seq
        print("exact safe n=%d score=%.9f" % (candidates[safe_idx][1], val), flush=True)

    ranked = []
    for i, item in enumerate(candidates):
        ranked.append((item[0], i))
    ranked.sort()

    for score, idx in ranked:
        if evaluated >= 4:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > hard_end - 12.0:
            break
        if idx == safe_idx:
            continue
        n = candidates[idx][1]
        if n > 16500:
            continue
        if best_exact < np.inf and score > best_exact + 0.06:
            continue
        seq = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in candidates[idx][2]]
        val = _exact_score(seq)
        evaluated += 1
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact:
            best_exact = val
            best_seq = seq
        print("exact n=%d score=%.9f internal=%.9f" % (n, val, score), flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_item = candidates[0] if candidates else None
        if best_item is None:
            best_item = (0.0, 8192, _normalize_array(np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64)))
        for item in candidates[1:]:
            if item[0] < best_item[0]:
                best_item = item
        arr = _normalize_array(best_item[2])
        best_seq = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in arr]

    s = sum(best_seq)
    if s < 0.01:
        best_seq = [0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25]

    print("final length=%d best_exact=%.9f elapsed=%.1f" % (len(best_seq), best_exact, time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)
    return best_seq
```
